### **4.3. Dynamic Rules Options**

#### **4.3.1. Description**
```

The **dynamicrulesoptions** submenu provides a list of available event sources and information about their

action triggers, that can be used in the **dynamicrules** submenu. Event sources and event actions that are

not activated do not appear in the list and cannot be added to rules in the **dynamicrules** submenu


**NOTE** This chapter applies to network cameras only.


**Access level**

|Action|Camera|
|---|---|
|view|Admin|


#### **4.3.2. Syntax**

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw-cgi/eventrules.cgi?msubmenu=
 dynamicrulesoptions &action=<value>[&<parameter>=<value>]

```

318 Event


#### **4.3.3. Parameters**


























|Action|Parameter|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|view|Channel|REQ, RES|<csv>|Channel ID|
||Language|REQ|<enum>|Language of the interface to the<br>event type|
||EventSource.#.Type|RES|<string>|Event types provided by the<br>device|
||EventSource.#.Type_<<br>Language>|RES|<string>|Interface language data for the<br>language selected in the<br>Language parameter<br>Displayed only when the<br>**Language** parameter is specified|
||EventSource.#.Status|RES|<enum><br>Inactive, Active|Indicates whether the event is<br>currently active|
||EventSource.#.Policy|RES|<enum><br>OneShot, Property,<br>None|Indicates the event policy|
||EventSource.#.Action<br>Types|RES|<csv><br>GoToPreset,<br>AlarmOutput.#, SMTP,<br>EventPush, EventSpot,<br>FTP, AudioClip,<br>Record, Handover,<br>MQTTPublication,<br>LightAlarm|Event action types provided by<br>the device|
||EventSource.#.Rule.#.<br>Name|RES|<string>|The name of the rule in the event|
||AppEventSource.#.Ap<br>pName|RES|<string>|The name of the app installed<br>through the device’s opensdk|
||AppEventSource.#.Ty<br>pe|RES|<string>|Event types provided the opensdk<br>app of the device|
||AppEventSource.#.Ty<br>pe_<Language>|RES|<string>|Interface language data for the<br>language selected in the<br>Language parameter<br>Displayed only when the<br>**Language** parameter is specified|
||AppEventSource.#.Sta<br>tus|RES|<enum><br>Inactive, Active|Indicates whether the event of<br>the app is currently active|

|Action|Parameter|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
||AppEventSource.#.Pol<br>icy|RES|<enum><br>OneShot, Property,<br>None|'Oneshot’ means this event<br>doesn’t support<br>"EventSource.#.State" in the<br>dynamicrules submenu. On the<br>other side, 'Property' means the<br>opposite.|
||AppEventSource.#.Act<br>ionTypes|RES|<csv><br>GoToPreset,<br>AlarmOutput.#, SMTP,<br>EventPush, EventSpot,<br>FTP, AudioClip,<br>Record, Handover,<br>MQTTPublication,<br>LightAlarm|Event types provided by the<br>device for the event source of the<br>app|

### **4.4. Handover**

#### **4.4.1. Description**
```

The handover feature allows the user to redirect to the preset for the inter-operation of the PTZ camera(s)

when motion or a video analytics, tampering, or audio analysis event occurs. The **handover2** submenu

configures the receiver (PTZ) cameras to which the user will be redirected when motion or a video

analytics, tampering, or audio analysis event occurs.


**NOTE** This chapter applies to network cameras and encoder only.


**Access level**

|Action|Camera|Encoder|
|---|---|---|
|view|Admin|Admin|
|set|Admin|Admin|
|add/update|Admin|Admin|
|remove|Admin|Admin|


#### **4.4.2. Syntax**

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw-cgi/eventrules.cgi?msubmenu=
 handover &action=<value>[&<parameter>=<value>]

#### **4.4.3. Parameters**

```








|Action|Parameter|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|view||||Reads handover settings|


328 Event


|Action|Parameter|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
||Channel|REQ, RES|<int>|Channel ID|
||ROIIndex|REQ, RES|<int>|Index of ROI(Region of Interest)|
||PresetIndex|REQ|<csv><br>**All**, #|Index of the preset:<br>• All – Get handover information for<br>all presets<br>• # - Get handover information for<br>specific preset<br>**Note**<br>**PresetIndex** parameter is valid<br>only for PTZ cameras.<br>For PTZ cameras,**PresetIndex** is<br>not passed in view action; camera<br>provides global handover<br>information.|
|set|Channel|REQ, RES|<int>|Channel ID|
||PresetIndex|REQ, RES|<int>|Index of the preset<br>**Note**<br>**PresetIndex** parameter is valid<br>only for PTZ cameras.|
||ROIIndex|REQ, RES|<int>|Index of ROI(Region of Interest)|
||Enable|REQ, RES|<bool><br>True, False|Enable or disable handover for the<br>specified ROI<br>**Note**<br>**ROIIndex** should be sent along<br>with**Enable** parameter.<br>For PTZ cameras,**PresetIndex** and<br>**ROIIndex** should be sent along<br>with**Enable** parameter.|
|add/update|Channel|REQ, RES|<int>|Channel ID|
||PresetIndex|REQ, RES|<int>|Index of the preset<br>**Note**<br>**PresetIndex** parameter is valid<br>only for PTZ cameras.|
||ROIIndex|REQ, RES|<int>|Index of ROI(Region of Interest)|


SUNAPI 329


|Action|Parameter|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
||HandoverIndex|REQ, RES|<int>|Index of receiver camera<br>**Note**<br>**ROIIndex** should be sent along<br>with**HandoverIndex** parameter.<br>For PTZ cameras,**PresetIndex** and<br>**ROIIndex** should be sent along<br>with**HandoverIndex** parameter.|
||IPType|REQ, RES|<enum><br>IPV4, IPV6|IP Type of receiver camera|
||IPAddress|REQ, RES|<string><br><formatInf<br>o="IPv4Add<br>ress or<br>IPv6Addres<br>s>|IP Address of receiver camera|
||Port|REQ, RES|<int>|Port of receiver camera|
||Username|REQ, RES|<string>|User name of receiver camera|
||Password|REQ, RES|<string>|Password of receiver camera|
||PresetNumber|REQ, RES|<int>|Preset number of receiver camera|
||IsPasswordEncrypted|REQ|<bool><br>True, False|Enables or disables password<br>encryption for receiver camera|
|remove|Channel|REQ|<int>|Channel ID|
||PresetIndex|REQ|<int>|Index of the preset|
||ROIIndex|REQ|<int>|Index of ROI(Region of Interest) to be<br>deleted|
||HandoverIndex|REQ|<csv>|Index of receiver camera to be deleted|

#### **4.4.6. Setting handover**
```

**Enabling MD handover for region 1**


REQUEST

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw cgi/eventrules.cgi?msubmenu=handover&action=set&ROIIndex=1&Enable=True

```

For PTZ cameras, PresetIndex should be passed along with ROIIndex to enable preset-based MD

handover.


REQUEST

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw-cgi/eventrules.cgi?msubmenu=handover&action=set&
 &PresetIndex=1&ROIIndex=1&Enable=True

```

SUNAPI 333


**NOTE** PTZ camera supports both Global MD handover & Preset-based MD handover.

#### **4.4.7. Configuring receiver camera(s)**

A receiver camera can be added to the MD ROI by providing the IPType, IPAddress, Port, Username,

Password, and PresetNumber of the receiver camera.


REQUEST

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw cgi/eventrules.cgi?msubmenu=handover&action=add&PresetIndex=1&ROIIndex=1&IPT
 ype=IPv4&IPAddress=1.1.1.1&Port=80&Username=admin&Password=4321&PresetNumber
 =1

```

For PTZ cameras, **PresetIndex** should be passed along with ROIIndex while adding receiver camera for

preset based MD handover.


REQUEST

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw cgi/eventrules.cgi?msubmenu=handover&action=add&PresetIndex=1&ROIIndex=1&IPT
 ype=IPv4&IPAddress=1.1.1.1&Port=80&Username=admin&Password=4321&PresetNumber
 =1

```

**HandoVerIndex** is used to update receiver camera details to specific MD ROI.


REQUEST

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw cgi/eventrules.cgi?msubmenu=handover&action=update&PresetIndex=1&ROIIndex=1&
 HandoverIndex=1&IPType=IPv4&IPAddress=1.1.1.2&Port=80&Username=admin&Passwor
 d=4321&PresetNumber=1

#### **4.4.8. Removing receiver camera(s)**
```

**HandoverIndex** is used to remove Receiver camera from the specific MD ROI.


REQUEST

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw-cgi/eventrules.cgi?msubmenu=handover&action=remove&
 &ROIIndex=1

```

For PTZ cameras, **PresetIndex** should be passed along with ROIIndex while removing receiver camera

from preset-based MD handover.


334 Event


REQUEST

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw-cgi/eventrules.cgi?msubmenu=handover&action=remove&
 &ROIIndex=1&PresetIndex=1

### **4.5. Handover 2**

#### **4.5.1. Description**
```

The 'handover2' feature allows the user to redirect to the preset for the inter-operation of PTZ camera(s)

when motion or video analytics or a tampering or audio analysis event occurs. The **handover2** submenu

configures the receiver (PTZ) cameras to which the user will be redirected when a motion or video

analytics or a tampering or audio analysis event occurs.


This chapter applies to network cameras and encoder only.



**NOTE**


**Access level**



Attributes to check handover2 feature support: stw
cgi/attributes.cgi/eventrules/handover2



|Action|Camera|Encoder|
|---|---|---|
|view|Admin|Admin|
|set|Admin|Admin|
|add/update|Admin|Admin|
|remove|Admin|Admin|

#### **4.5.2. Syntax**

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw-cgi/eventrules.cgi?msubmenu=
 handover2 &action=<value>[&<parameter>=<value>]

#### **4.5.3. Parameters**

```








|Action|Parameter|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|view||||Reads handover settings|
||Channel|REQ, RES|<int>|Channel ID|
|add/update|Channel|REQ, RES|<int>|Channel ID|
||HandoverIndex|REQ, RES|<int>|Index of receiver camera|


SUNAPI 335


|Action|Parameter|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
||IPType|REQ, RES|<enum><br>IPV4, IPV6,<br>DomainNa<br>me|IP Type of receiver camera|
||IPAddress|REQ, RES|<string><br><formatInf<br>o="IPv4Add<br>ress or<br>IPv6Addres<br>s>|IP Address of receiver camera|
||Port|REQ, RES|<int>|Port of receiver camera|
||ConnectionMode|REQ, RES|<enum><br>HTTP,<br>HTTPS, TCP|Type of handover target connection|
||Username|REQ, RES|<string>|User name of receiver camera|
||Password|REQ, RES|<string>|Password of receiver camera|
||Action|REQ, RES|<enum><br>Preset,<br>Custom|Action type of handover<br>• Preset : Request to move to the<br>preset<br>• Custom: In case of HTTP/HTTPS,<br>request the set URL + Query. In<br>case of TCP, send a message.|
||PresetNumber|REQ, RES|<int>|Preset number of receiver camera|
||Query|REQ, RES|<string>|Available only for HTTP/HTTPS. Query<br>string to request in URL|
||Message|REQ, RES|<string>|Available only for TCP. Message to be<br>transmitted over TCP|
||IsPasswordEncrypted|REQ|<bool><br>True, False|Enables or disables password<br>encryption for receiver camera|
||ConnectionMode|REQ, RES|<enum><br>HTTP,<br>HTTPS|Connection mode to connect receiver<br>camera|
|remove|Channel|REQ|<int>|Channel ID|
||HandoverIndex|REQ|<csv>|Index of receiver camera to be deleted|

PresetNumber of the receiver camera along with **HandoverIndex**, **IsPasswordEncrypted** parameters.

```

### **4.6. Scheduler**

#### **4.6.1. Description**
```

The **scheduler** submenu configures the schedule settings for report generation for people count and heat

map.


**NOTE** This chapter applies to network cameras only.


**Access level**

|Action|Camera|
|---|---|
|view|Admin|
|set|Admin|


#### **4.6.2. Syntax**

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw-cgi/eventrules.cgi?msubmenu=
 scheduler &action=<value>[&<parameter>=<value>]

```

SUNAPI 339


#### **4.6.3. Parameters**



















|Action|Parameter|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|view||||Reads scheduler settings|
||Channel|REQ, RES|<int>|Channel ID|
||Type|REQ, RES|<enum><br>PeopleCou<br>nt,<br>HeatMap,<br>QueueMan<br>agement,<br>VehicleCou<br>nt|Feature Type|
|set|Channel|REQ, RES|<int>|Channel ID|
||Type|REQ, RES|<enum><br>PeopleCou<br>nt,<br>HeatMap,<br>QueueMan<br>agement,<br>VehicleCou<br>nt|Feature Type|
||ScheduleType|REQ, RES|<enum><br>Daily,<br>Weekly|Type of schedule.|
||WeekDay|REQ, RES|<enum><br>SUN, MON,<br>TUE, WED,<br>THU, FRI,<br>SAT|Day of the week<br>**Note**<br>This parameter is valid only when<br>**ScheduleType** set to Weekly.|
||Hour|REQ, RES|<int>|Hour of the day|
||Minute|REQ, RES|<int>|Minute at which report will be<br>generated|


340 Event


|Action|Parameter|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
||EventAction|REQ, RES|<csv><br>AlarmOutp<br>ut.#, SMTP,<br>FTP,<br>Record,<br>HTTP,<br>GoToPreset|Event action<br>• AlarmOutput.#: Activates alarm<br>output when the configured event<br>occurs.<br>• SMTP: Sends notification and<br>image as an attachment via SMTP<br>when the configured event occurs.<br>• FTP: Uploads image via FTP when<br>the configured event occurs.<br>• Record: Saves event video on the<br>storage device when the<br>configured event occurs. (Refer to<br>'Recording' document (of<br>**recording.cgi**) for information on<br>configuring recording settings<br>such as pre and post event<br>buffers)<br>• GoToPreset: Moves to the<br>specified preset position when the<br>configured event occurs.<br>**Note**<br>**transfer.cgi** is used to configure<br>FTP/SMTP server settings.|
||AlarmOutput.#.Duration|REQ, RES|<enum><br>Always, 5s,<br>10s, 15s|Alarm output duration when the event<br>occurs<br>**AlarmOutput.#.Duration** is valid only<br>when**EventAction** is set to<br>AlarmOutput.|

### **4.7. Schedulelist**

#### **4.7.1. Description**
```

The **schedulelist** submenu used to manage several schedulers.

|---|---|

|update|User|
|remove|User|

||Schedule.#.TUE|RES|<string>|Array of space-separated 1 and 0 for<br>each hour of the day.<br>• 1: Enabled for that hour<br>• 0: Disabled for that hour|

### **4.8. Audio Clip**

#### **4.8.1. Description**
```

The **audiooutfiles** submenu manages audio clip files which are used as a sort of event notification.


**NOTE** This chapter applies to network cameras only.


**Access level**

|Action|Camera|AMS|
|---|---|---|
|view|Admin|User|
|set|Admin||
|install|Admin|Admin|
|control|Admin|Admin|
|remove|Admin|Admin|


#### **4.8.2. Syntax**

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw-cgi/eventrules.cgi?msubmenu=
 audiooutfiles &action=<value>[&<parameter>=<value>]

#### **4.8.3. Parameters**

```

356 Event


|Action|Parameter|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|view|TotalSpace|RES|<string>|Total space of unit as KB|
||FreeSpace|RES|<string>|Free space of unit as KB|
|set|Index|REQ, RES|<int>|Index of audio clip|
||Gain|REQ, RES|<int>|Audio output gain<br>The value is in the range of 1 to 5.|
||Name|REQ, RES|<string>|File name of audio clip|
|install|Index|REQ, RES|<int>|Index of audio clip|
||Name|REQ, RES|<string>|File name of audio clip|
||Type|REQ, RES|<enum><br>WAV|File type of audio clip|
||DeviceID|REQ|<int>|Device ID where the audio file should<br>be stored.<br>**Note**<br>refer**registeredsubdevices**<br>response for the device ids with<br>AudioSourceType**BGM** support.<br>**AMS ONLY**<br>|
||Gain|REQ, RES|<int>|Audio output gain<br>The value is in the range of 1 to 5.|
|control|Type|REQ|<enum><br>Donwload,<br>Play, Stop|Control type<br>• Download: Downloads the audio<br>clip to the client<br>• Play: Plays the audio clip<br>• Stop: Stops playing of the audio<br>clip<br>**Note**<br>Download not supported in AMS|
||Index|REQ|<int>|Index of audio clip**CAMERA ONLY**<br>|
||Name|REQ|<string>|Unique name of the audio file<br>**AMS ONLY**<br>|
||SpeakerID|REQ|<int>|Speaker ID where the audio should be<br>played<br>**AMS ONLY**<br>|


SUNAPI 357


|Action|Parameter|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
||GroupID|REQ|<int>|Speaker Group ID where the audio<br>should be played<br>**AMS ONLY**<br>|
||Volume|REQ|<int><br>1~100|Volume in which the audio file needs<br>to be played.<br>**AMS ONLY**<br>|
||LoopCount|REQ|<int><br>1~99|Audio repeat count, if not passed<br>LoopCount is considered as 1.<br>**AMS ONLY**<br>|
|remove|Index|REQ, RES|<int>|Index of audio clip|
||Name|REQ|<string>|Unique name of the audio file<br>**AMS ONLY**<br>|

### **4.9. TTS (Text to speech) Files**

#### **4.9.1. Description**
```

The **ttsfiles** submenu manages tts files in the device.


**NOTE** This chapter applies to only AMS.


**Access level**

|Action|AMS|
|---|---|
|view|User|



SUNAPI 361


|Action|AMS|
|---|---|
|install|Admin|
|control|Admin|
|remove|Admin|

#### **4.9.2. Syntax**

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw cgi/eventrules.cgi?msubmenu=ttsfiles&action=<value>[&<parameter>=<value>]

#### **4.9.3. Parameters**

```













|Action|Parameter|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|view|TTSList.#.Index|RES|<int>|Index of the tts file|
||TTSList.#.Name|RES|<string>|File name of the tts file|
|install|Name|REQ|<string>|File name of tts file (unique name<br>needs to be provided)|
||DeviceID|REQ|<int>|Device ID where the tts file should be<br>stored.<br>**Note**<br>refer**registeredsubdevices**<br>response for the device ids with<br>AudioSourceType**TTS** support.|
||Language|REQ|<enum><br>English-US,<br>English-UK,<br>Korean,<br>French,<br>Spanish,<br>German,<br>Russian|Language of the TTS file|
||Gender|REQ|<enum><br>Male,<br>Female|Language of the TTS file|
||Pitch|REQ|<int><br>50~200|Pitch to be used when playing file.<br>Default value is 100.|
||Speed|REQ|<int><br>50~400|Text file playback speed. Default value<br>is 100.|



362 Event


|Action|Parameter|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
||DelayBetweenSentence|REQ|<int><br>0~65536|Delay in between two sentenses in<br>millisecs.Default is 500 ms.|
||DelayBetweenComma|REQ|<int><br>0~65536|Delay for comma, in millisecs.Default<br>is 300 ms.|
|control|Type|REQ|<enum><br>Play, Stop|Control type<br>• Play: Plays the tts file<br>• Stop: Stops playing of the tts file<br>**Note**<br>When sending**stop** command,<br>same tts filename and SpeakerID<br>or GroupID needs to be passed<br>that was used in**play** command.|
||Name|REQ|<string>|Unique name of the tts file|
||SpeakerID|REQ|<int>|Speaker ID where the audio should be<br>played|
||GroupID|REQ|<int>|Speaker Group ID where the audio<br>should be played|
||Volume|REQ|<int><br>1~100|Volume in which the tts file needs to<br>be played.|
||LoopCount|REQ|<int><br>1~99|Audio repeat count, if not passed<br>LoopCount is considered as 1.|
|remove|Name|REQ|<string>|Unique name of the tts file|

#### **4.9.4. Examples** **4.9.5. Getting list of tts files**

 http://<Device IP>/stw-cgi/eventrules.cgi?msubmenu=ttsfiles&action=view

```
 HTTP/1.0 200 OK
 Content-type: application/json
 <Body>

```

### **4.11. Audio Clip Schedule**

#### **4.11.1. Description**

The **audioclipschedule** submenu configures the schedule settings for audio clip playback.


**NOTE** This chapter applies to network cameras only.


**Access level**

|Action|Camera|
|---|---|
|view|Admin|
|set|Admin|


#### **4.11.2. Syntax**

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw-cgi/eventrules.cgi?msubmenu=
 audiooutfliesschedule &action=<value>[&<parameter>=<value>]

#### **4.11.3. Parameters**

```



















|Action|Parameter|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|view|Channel|REQ, RES|<int>|Channel ID|
|set|Channel|REQ, RES|<int>|Channel ID|
||Enable|REQ, RES|<bool><br>True, False|Enables or disables playback<br>activation<br>• True: Activated<br>• False: Deactivated|
||AudioClipeIndex|REQ, RES|<int>|Index of audio clip|
||Hour|REQ, RES|<int>|Hour of the day|
||Minute|REQ, RES|<int>|Minute at which report will be<br>generated|
||WeekDay|REQ, RES|<enum><br>SUN, MON,<br>TUE, WED,<br>THU, FRI,<br>SAT|Day of the week<br>**Note**<br>This parameter is valid only when<br>**ScheduleType** is set to Weekly.|



370 Event


|Action|Parameter|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
||ScheduleType|REQ, RES|<enum><br>Daily,<br>Weekly|Time schedule to play audio clip<br>• Daily: At scheduled time every day<br>• Weekly: At scheduled day and time|

### **4.12. Internal Handover Calibration**

#### **4.12.1. Description**
```

The **internalhandovercalibration** submenu manages the internal handover calibration settings which

are used for the smart zoom feature.


**NOTE** This chapter is applicable for multi-directional cameras that have a PTZ channel.


**Access level**

|Action|Camera|
|---|---|
|view|Admin|
|set|Admin|
|control|Admin|
|check|Admin|
|remove|Admin|

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw cgi/eventrules.cgi?msubmenu= internalhandovercalibration &action=<value>[&<par
 ameter>=<value>]

```

|Action|Parameter|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|

|view|Channel|REQ|<int>|Channel ID|
|set|Channel|REQ, RES|<int>|Channel ID|
||PTZChannel.#.Mapping.#<br>.LocalScreenCoordinate|REQ, RES|<string>|Target Channel’s coordinates that PTZ<br>Channel would head for. (X, Y)|
||PTZChannel.#.Mapping.#<br>.PTCoordinate|REQ, RES|<string>|Absolute coordinates of Pan and Tilt of<br>PTZ channels to calibrate with target<br>channel. (P, T)|
|control|Channel|REQ|<int>|Channel ID|

||CalibrationCompleted|RES|<bool><br>True, False|Shows whether each channel’s<br>calibration settings are complete or<br>not|
|remove|Channel|RES|<int>|Channel ID|
||PTZChannels|RES|<csv><br>#, All|PTZ Channel ID|

### **4.14. indicationpass**

#### **4.14.1. Description**

The **indicationpass** submenu allows the user to pass event information to a predefined camera when a

parking detection event occurs. A fisheye lens is not good for watching a parking area just below the

camera, so the user can set it to observe the opposite side of parking lot to detect parked vehicles and

send analyzed data to the opposite camera. This feature will work only when the user sets the LEDUsage

value in ledindicator submenu to 1.


**Access level**

|Action|Camera|Encoder|
|---|---|---|
|view|Admin|Admin|
|add/update|Admin|Admin|
|remove|Admin|Admin|


#### **4.14.2. Syntax**

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw-cgi/eventrules.cgi?msubmenu=
 indicationpass &action=view&[&<parameter>=<value>...]

#### **4.14.3. Parameters**

```














|Action|Parameter|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|view||||Reads indication pass settings|
||Channel|REQ, RES|<int>|Channel ID|
|add/update|Channel|REQ, RES|<int>|Channel ID|
||IndicationPassIndex|REQ, RES|<int>|Index of receiver camera|
||IPType|REQ, RES|<enum><br>IPV4, IPV6|IP Type of receiver camera|
||IPAddress|REQ, RES|<string><br><formatInf<br>o="IPv4Add<br>ress or<br>IPv6Addres<br>s>|IP Address of receiver camera|
||Port|REQ, RES|<int>|Port of receiver camera|
||Username|REQ, RES|<string>|User name of receiver camera|
||Password|REQ, RES|<string>|Password of receiver camera|

## **Chapter 5. Event Status**
### **5.1. Event Status**

#### **5.1.1. Description**

**eventstatus.cgi** is used to request the current event status (activated or deactivated).


**eventstatus.cgi** has three submenus — check, monitor and monitordiff: The **check** submenu requests the

current status once; The **monitor** submenu requests the current status on a certain time interval,

irrespective of whether the state changed or not (and also requests the event status if an event occurs);

and The **monitordiff** submenu initially requests the status of all events and later makes requests

whenever any change from previous state occurs.


**Access level**

|Action|Camera|Encoder|NVR|
|---|---|---|---|
|check|Guest|Guest|User|
|monitor|Guest|Guest|User|
|monitordiff|Guest|Guest|User|


#### **5.1.2. Syntax**

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw-cgi/eventstatus.cgi?msubmenu=
 eventstatus &action=<value>[&<parameter>=<value>]

#### **5.1.3. Parameters**

```

#### **5.1.6. Monitoring status**
```

The **monitor** submenu requests the current event status on a certain time interval (specified with the

**Periodicity** parameter), irrespective of whether the event occurred or not (state changed or not).


396 Event


The status is represented with the value True (for activated) or False (for deactivated).


Continuously monitoring the status of face detection events on Channel 0 and the alarm input


REQUEST

#### **5.1.7. Requesting changed events**
```

The **monitordiff** submenu initially requests the status of all events and later makes requests only when an

event occurs (change from previous state), which is different from the **monitor** submenu requesting the

event status periodically.


The status is represented with the value True (for activated) or False (for deactivated).


Requesting changed status motion detection events for Channel 0 only


REQUEST

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw cgi/eventstatus.cgi?msubmenu=eventstatus&action=monitordiff&Channel.0.EventT
 ype=MotionDetection

```

```
 HTTP/1.1 200 OK
 Content-Type: multipart/x-mixed-replace; boundary=SamsungTechwin

 Channel.0.MotionDetection.RegionID.1=False
 Channel.0.MotionDetection.RegionID.1.Level=0

### **5.2. Push Notification**

#### **5.2.1. Description**
```

The **Pushnotification** submenu is used to configure push notification feature in NVR.


**NOTE** This chapter applies to the NVR only.


**Access level**

|Action|NVR|
|---|---|
|view|User|
|set|User|


#### **5.2.2. Syntax**

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw-cgi/eventstatus.cgi?msubmenu=
 pushnotification &action=<value>[&<parameter>=<value>]

#### **5.2.3. Parameters**

```










|Action|Parameter|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|view||||Reads current pushnotification<br>settings|
|set|Enable|REQ, RES|<bool><br>True, False|Enables or disables push notifications|

#### **5.2.4. Examples** **5.2.5. Getting the current pushnotification settings**

REQUEST

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw
```



```
 cgi/eventstatus.cgi?msubmenu=pushnotification&action=view

```

TEXT RESPONSE

```
 HTTP/1.0 200 OK
 Content-type: text/plain

```

SUNAPI 419


```
 <Body>

 Enable=True

```

JSON RESPONSE

```
 HTTP/1.0 200 OK
 Content-type: application/json
 <Body>

 {

 "Enable":true

 }

#### **5.2.6. Setting the current pushnotification settings**
```

REQUEST

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw cgi/eventstatus.cgi?msubmenu=pushnotification&action=set&Enable=False

### **5.3. ONVIF Event Topic**

#### **5.3.1. Description**
```

This **eventscheme** submenu is used to choose whether old or new topics should be used in the ONVIF

event service.


This submenu is applicable for network camera & encoder only. Based on this setting, the
**NOTE**

onvif event service **GetEventProperties** response will change.


**Access level**

|Action|Camera|Encoder|
|---|---|---|
|view|Admin|Admin|
|set|Admin|Admin|



420 Event


#### **5.3.2. Syntax**

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw-cgi/eventstatus.cgi?msubmenu=
 eventscheme &action=<value>[&<parameter>=<value>]

#### **5.3.3. Parameters**

```










|Action|Parameter|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|view||||Reads current ONVIF event topic<br>settings|
|set|Type|REQ, RES|<enum><br>Proprietary,<br>ONVIF|When set to Proprietary it will follow<br>the old topic set; when set to ONVIF<br>the new standard topics set will be<br>used.|

#### **5.3.4. Examples**

**5.3.4.1. Getting the current eventscheme**

REQUEST




```
 http://<Device IP>/stw-cgi/eventstatus.cgi?msubmenu=eventscheme&action=view

```

### **5.4. Metadataschema**

#### **5.4.1. Description**

This **metadataschema** submenu provides schema of metadata events generated from the camera when

an event occurs.


**NOTE** This submenu is applicable for network camera.


**Access level**

|Action|Camera|
|---|---|
|view|Admin|


#### **5.4.2. Syntax**

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw-cgi/eventstatus.cgi?msubmenu=
 metadataschema &action=<value>[&<parameter>=<value>]

#### **5.4.3. Parameters**

```








|Action|Parameter|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|view||||Reads current metadata schema<br>settings|


422 Event


|Action|Parameter|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
||EventName|REQ, RES|<enum><br>AlarmInput,<br>AlarmOutput,<br>MotionDetection,<br>VideoAnalytics,<br>VideoLoss,<br>FaceDetection,<br>AudioDetection,<br>AudioAnalytics,<br>Tampering,<br>DefocusDetection,<br>ShockDetection,Temp<br>eratureChangeDetecti<br>on, FogDetection,<br>Tracking,<br>BoxTemperatureDete<br>ction,<br>ObjectDetection,<br>MaskDetection,<br>BodyTemperatureDet<br>ection,<br>ParkingDetection,<br>SocialDistancingViolat<br>ion, CallRequest,<br>TamperingSwitch,<br>DTMFReceived,<br>ProximitySensor|Shows the event name’s<br>metadata schema.|
||EventTopic|RES|<string>|ONVIF event topic associated with<br>the Event|
||EventSchema|RES|<string>|ONVIF event schema|

### **5.5. Event Status Schema**

#### **5.5.1. Description**
```

This **eventstatusschema** submenu provides event schema of all events. This schema is applied in the

evenstatus submenu with SchemaBased parameters.


**NOTE** This submenu is applicable for network cameras and NVR only.


**Access level**

|Action|Camera|NVR|
|---|---|---|
|view|Admin|User|


#### **5.5.2. Syntax**

```
 http://<Device IP>/stw-cgi/eventstatus.cgi?msubmenu=
 eventstatusschema &action=<value>[&<parameter>=<value>]

#### **5.5.3. Parameters**

```








|Action|Parameter|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|view||||Reads current event status<br>schema settings|


428 Event


|Action|Parameter|Request/<br>Response|Type/<br>Value|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|
||EventName|REQ, RES|<enum><br>AlarmInput,<br>AlarmOutput,<br>MotionDetection,<br>FaceDetection,<br>VideoLoss,<br>Tampering,<br>AudioDetection,<br>DefocusDetection,<br>FogDetection,<br>ShockDetection,<br>Tracking, PTZMotion,<br>DigitalAutoTracking,<br>VideoAnalytics,<br>AudioAnalytics,<br>Queue, SystemEvent,<br>ConfigChange,<br>TemperatureChangeD<br>etection,<br>BoxTemperatureDete<br>ction,<br>ObjectDetection,<br>OpenSDK,<br>BodyTemperatureDet<br>ection,ParkingDetecti<br>on,<br>SocialDistancingViolat<br>ion, CallRequest,<br>TamperingSwitch,<br>DTMFReceived,<br>ProximitySensor,<br>MQTTSubscription|This parameter can only show the<br>requested value’s response.|
||Language|REQ|<enum>|Requests the translated value for<br>event topics.<br>**Note**<br>This can be applicable only<br>when<br>**MultiLanguageEventSchem**<br>**a** is True in attributes.<br>**CAMERA ONLY**<br>|
